Mutations (scratch kit, rev39 tests): M1 MERGE_HEAD only (13 fail), M2 no record after the command (2), M3 a record of any mtime counts (2), M4 continue/abort not the sync's own work (1), M5 no grant wait on a revert (3), M6 a revert ends with commit (3), M7 takeover writes no record (5), M8 the notice says merge (2), M9 the freeze holder does not outrank a record (1), M10 the guard says merge (3), M11 a record after any git write (1), M12 a record with no head file (1), M13 an open sync stops the head takeover (4), M14 a live owner taken over (2). The branch fast-forwards onto intent/a5r (`git merge-base --is-ancestor intent/a5r HEAD`, intent/a5r at c4d2223). Acceptance: A115 met; finding F-9 resolved.
- S120 (rev 39, 2026-10-21): the Fable adversary review of 4b51c86/999117e ("fix first", 569 pass, one timing flake on the first run; measured on git 2.45.1: a rebase leaves REBASE_HEAD and rebase-merge/apply, never CHERRY_PICK_HEAD; status, checkout --ours/--theirs, add and diff leave the head file's mtime alone; --continue or --skip onto the next conflict writes a new head file; --quit and git commit remove it, .git/sequencer stays for a multi-commit pick), committed e799ddd. Gates before (999117e, code 4b51c86): `✔ Validation passed`, tsc exit 0, `569 pass 0 fail`, scan exit 0, smoke exit 0. Fixes: (D1, medium-high) a coinciding pull or merge took the owner record (`noteHeadOwner` claimed whenever the record was not its own) → the command's start is taken before `next(e)` (`opStartOf`), and opguard.ts `mayClaim(h, op, rec, coordinates)` claims only when the session coordinates, one of the command's own operations can have left that head file (gitshared `OPS.leftBy`, `isLeftBy`: a pull leaves MERGE_HEAD only), the file was written after the command started, and no record is valid for it (a valid record is never overwritten); `claimHead` only reads and writes. (D2, medium) the notice skipped every open phase while A61 waits in every phase → `mergeGuardStep` skips only the freeze; `NOTICES.mergeLeft` takes the phase and says "during the sync at HH:MM by X (phase)" (`syncWhere`), with no "then plan a new sync". (D3, low) `moveAfterClear` moves the owner record to the new session id through `writeHeadOwner`, the one checked writer. (D4, low) a backgrounded (`run_in_background`) operation is kept (`opRunning`, `withOp` merges two: the verbs together, the earlier start) and claimed by `claimStep` at the minute; it is forgotten once a git of the checkout was read running and then none is (seen, then gone: a probe older than the command never ends it), else after 2 h. (D5, low) documented, not built: a session outside the checkout (editorLock set, root without the .uproject) has no session file and no tick, so no liveness: a record naming it could never be taken over; it is guarded like every session, never recorded owner (`mayClaim`'s `coordinates`, and `op` only for a coordinating session), and its takeover answers "no session file" as before (README Giới hạn). (D6) documented, not built: `.git/sequencer` left after `git commit` mid-sequence has no head file to tie an owner to, a forgotten one would block every session though git blocks nothing, and the next `--continue` that conflicts writes a new head file, which is guarded again (README Giới hạn); so the worker's rule 4 is unchanged. Thermo-nuclear review of the new diff (vs 4b51c86, in the context of the branch): pass 1 changes requested, (R1) dropping the `isS2` gate let a non-coordinating session that an A5R tool had given a session file be recorded owner, then look gone → the gate is part of the claim rule (`coordinates`), test; (C1) the claim rule sat inline in register.ts → opguard.ts `OpStart`, `withOp`, `mayClaim` with a unit table; non-critical applied: claimStep on facts (the probe) instead of a bare 2-h window, two pending operations merged, one writer for the record, `syncWhere`. Pass 2 approve, with a should-fix: claimStep could drop a backgrounded operation on a reading older than the command (or a git without -C) → seen, then gone, and `op` gated on isS2 at the call site; test. Pass 3: see below. Left with reasons: the STARTS_OP pre-filter in `opStartOf` (redundant with `isLeftBy` in behaviour, it saves a locate and a stat on every git write: mutation M11 survives by design); two operations of the same kind started at once: the first record wins; a `cd <checkout> && git …` command is neither guarded nor recorded (gitWrites gives no folder, so it resolves to the session's cwd; as before this branch). Gates after ({SHA}): `✔ Validation passed`, tsc exit 0, `579 pass 0 fail`, scan exit 0, smoke exit 0. Mutations (scratch kit, rev39 and rev39b tests): N1 any command claims any head file (2 fail), N2 a valid record overwritten (2), N3 an older head file claimed (2), N4 no notice during a planned sync (1), N5 /clear leaves the record (1), N6 a backgrounded operation never claimed (3), N7 every operation kept for the minute (1), N8 a non-coordinating session claims (1), N9 kept after its git ended (1), N10 forgotten while its git runs (2), N11 forgotten when never seen (1); M1-M10, M13, M14 again (each fails). The branch fast-forwards onto intent/a5r (`git merge-base --is-ancestor intent/a5r HEAD`, intent/a5r at c4d2223).
- S121 (rev 40, 2026-10-22): the design of A116-A117, written before building them. Gates before (49dd7a7, types copied from the live plugin): `claude plugin validate a5r` → `✔ Validation passed`; `npx -y -p typescript@5.6 tsc -p a5r` exit 0; `claude plugin test a5r` → `579 pass 0 fail`; `node --experimental-strip-types a5r/tests/scan-old-names.mjs` exit 0; `node --experimental-strip-types a5r/tests/build-script-smoke.mjs` exit 0.

  The target (Hai's approved mockup, L-44):

  ```
   EDITOR · sync-main has it · 81 min left
   ▶ ♾️S2 sync main        sync 12:05 · Editor not open yet
   1 Hệ thống enemy chim mới   waiting 77 min · 45 min · PIE   behind sync-main
   2 AoBing fox form weapon    waiting 74 min · 75 min · PIE
   3 Limbshake trong Enemy S2  waiting 65 min · 45 min · build
   4 Enhance Form material     waiting 49 min · 15 min
     today: 25 turns · usually 38 min wait · 18% of turns spent opening the Editor

   OTHER SESSIONS · 10
   ★ f443b863 (this one)   no intent · 7m
   ● Walker topic S2 deep dive        working · 2m
   ● TALab flow skill set             idle 40m
     +7 more

   NEEDS A LOOK                        (only appears when there is something)
   ⚠ 11:59 sync-main: idle for 25 min while holding the Editor, likely stuck.
     → wake it, or release its turn
  ```

  The person-only controls (A117), in the same style. A row's mark (`▶`, its place number, `★` for this session) is a plain Button: a press (a click, or Enter where the terminal's focus ring sits on it) selects that row and shows its controls on one line under it; a second press, or a press on another row, moves the selection. The controls are plain Buttons with one-key hotkeys while the A5R pane holds the keys (the terminal draws them `u: Up`; the desktop draws native buttons; a click works on both):

  ```
   EDITOR · sync-main has it · 81 min left
   ▶ ♾️S2 sync main        sync 12:05 · Editor not open yet
   1 Hệ thống enemy chim mới   waiting 77 min · 45 min · PIE · behind sync-main
   2 AoBing fox form weapon    waiting 74 min · 75 min · PIE
       u: Up   d: Down   t: To top   m: −15 min   p: +15 min          ← row 2 selected
   3 Limbshake trong Enemy S2  waiting 65 min · 45 min · build · placed by hand
   ...

   ▶ AoBing fox form weapon    granted 12:05 · Editor 12:09
       1: +15 min   3: +30 min   6: +60 min                           ← the holder selected
  ```

  A row placed by the person reads `placed by hand`; minutes the person set read `30 min (set by hand)`. Every press answers in a toast (what changed, or why not); the slash commands answer in text: `/a5r editor` (the list and the commands), `/a5r editor up|down|top <name>`, `/a5r editor time <name> <+minutes|-minutes|minutes>`, `/a5r editor order reset`. `<name>` is a row's app title, lane or id8: an exact name first, else a unique prefix; two matches are refused naming both.

  Mechanics (coord.ts `queueOf`/`decide` stay pure and every session computes the order from the shared files):
  - `Saved/A5R/queue-order.json` (new pure module `hooks/order.ts`): `placed` (the person's order: id8, the request it lasts for = that session's `requestedAt` and kept mark, set at, by), `sized` (minutes the person set for a queued request, the same identity), `changes` (the last 10: at, by, what, the sessions it moved, for their notices). Written only by a person's press or typed command, through any session's A5R, by read-compare-write (read, decide, re-read: unchanged → write, read back: ours → done; else "another press changed the order at the same moment → press again"): two panels pressing at once never interleave half an order; the later press is refused and asks to be pressed again.
  - `queueOf` ranks: the sync holder first in a cutoff or freeze (unchanged); then the placed sessions, in the person's order, while their request lasts; then the rest first-asked-first-served (A104's kept place is its `requestedAt`, as today). A move pins the new order's prefix through the farthest row it changed (and every row pinned before), so "Up" on row 3 pins rows 1-3 and the rest stay FIFO behind them.
  - An entry lasts while that session still asks with the same request (same `requestedAt` and kept mark), at most 12 h: its next lease ends (the request is cleared at release), or it is withdrawn, or a forced release's kept place (another `requestedAt`, `isKept`) is a new turn, and the entry lapses; the order then returns to first-asked-first-served. Every write drops the entries that no longer last; readers ignore them anyway. A placed session that is gone (its file stale) drops out of the queue as before (`queueOf` keeps only live requests), its entry lapses with its request.
  - A113: a placed head is never passed (`headStateOf`: no pass and no memory-only head when the head is placed); a placed later row may pass a FIFO head as any row may; aging counts from `requestedAt` as before. A104: a kept place ranks among the FIFO rows by its `requestedAt` (before newer ones), always after the rows the person placed.
  - Minutes the person set for a queued request are read by `decide` (`slotOf`: `sized ?? want.minutes`, still capped at 30 min in an A113 pass slot), never written into that session's own file (one writer per file); the 45-min build window of `request` (A81) does not apply to them.
  - Resizing the holder: any session's A5R rewrites the holder's standard HELD line with the new end (`end=` and `until`, read-compare-write; the press's session is often not the holder's); the holder's own A5R adopts the end its HELD line says at its next minute (`leaseStep`: its HELD line still names it with the same grant and its end differs from `holding.end` by a minute or more → `holding.end` takes it), so its warnings, overrun notice, `notePid` and `extend` all use the new end, and its own writes never put the old end back. A person's resize counts as no extension (the one-extension limit, the 45-min build window and A113's 30-min pass cap are the editor tool's, not the person's). Refused: a hand-written lock line (its holder's A5R must turn it into a standard line first), the sync's own slot (it ends with the sync), a lease during another session's sync cutoff or freeze (the Editor is the sync's then), a new end past a planned sync's cutoff (move the sync first), an end under 5 min from now (release instead) or past 12 h from the grant.
  - Telling: each change is logged once by the pressing session (`rule=manual`, what, by the person); each session's A5R reads `changes` at its minute and tells itself once (`editor:manual:<at>`) when the change moved it (its place, its minutes or its lease end), as a quiet notice on its next tool result.
  - Person-only: no action of the editor tool changes the order (its schema keeps `request`, `release`, `extend`, `status`; an unknown action still answers status); the `/a5r editor …` changes run only for the person's own command (`origin.kind` `composer` or `bridge`; a plugin's, a peer's, a scheduled or a coordinator's run is refused); a model's Edit/Write of `queue-order.json`, or a shell command that writes it, is refused like the lock ("Editor order — Saved/A5R/queue-order.json is the person's …" → request the Editor with the editor tool and wait for your turn).
  - Mixed versions: a 0.23 session reads no `queue-order.json`: it ranks first-asked-first-served, so a 0.23 session at the FIFO head takes a free Editor even when the person placed a 0.24 session first (the lock's read-compare-write lets one of them take it; the other waits); a 0.23 holder does not adopt a resized end (its own warnings and its next `notePid` write use its file's end, which can put the old end back into the line); minutes set by hand for a 0.23 request are ignored at its grant. So the controls hold fully once every session runs 0.24.0 (release note).
  Evidence: this design, committed with S122. Acceptance: none yet.

## Every place A5R can refuse or ask (rev 27, A78; rev 28 rows A79–A82; rev 29 A83; rev 30 A84–A88; rev 32 A93–A95; rev 33 A96–A99; rev 34 A102; rev 38 A113; rev 39 A115)

<!-- One row per refusal or ask point in a5r/hooks (grep of blocked(, deny, $.ui.ask, decision( and noticeText call sites). Now: allow / allow + recorded / refuse + alternative / check (facts) / removed. -->

| Gate | Matched before (0.14.3) | Now (0.15.0) | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Rule 1 dialog (`askHai`, `$.ui.ask` with Allow once / Allow for this session / No) | every rule-1 ask in ask mode | removed | A73: never asks, never waits; bypass and away windows showed no dialog |
| "needs Hai's approval: ask Hai to run it" (deny mode) and "nobody could approve it now" (away) | every rule-1 ask | removed | A73 |
| Worker refused at rule 1 ("a worker does not ask Hai") | every rule-1 ask by a subagent | removed: same answer as the main loop | A73 |
| `--no-verify` | refuse | refuse + alternative (fix what the hook reports) | skipping hooks hides failures for everyone |
| `GIT_LFS_SKIP_SMUDGE=1` | refuse | refuse + alternative (let LFS fetch; or a worktree of your own) | leaves pointer text inside .uasset files |
| `git sparse-checkout set/add/init/reapply` in the shared checkout | refuse | refuse + alternative (a worktree of your own with its own layout) | removes tracked files for every session |
| Writes into the live a5r plugin folder (shell or edit) | refuse, also reads after `cd` into it | refuse + alternative (change the mod in a worktree of its repository) only for file-writing commands; reads, tests, type checks, builds and pushd/popd after `cd`, other worktrees and temp paths named a5r run; rev 30 (A85, S88): any command naming the live kit is refused except the read-only checks (`claude plugin test|validate <kit>`, `tsc -p`/`--noEmit`, `bun test`/`node --test` on its tests, `git -C <kit> log|status|diff|show`, the readers rg/grep/cat/ls/head/tail/Get-Content/Select-String); a redirect into the kit refused for them too | A76, review 8, A85; the live folder hot-reloads into every session |
| Secret in an edit | refuse | refuse + alternative (env variable or config outside the repository) | irrecoverable once pushed |
| Force push (`--force`, `-f`, `+refspec`) | ask (the pattern also matched `--force-with-lease`) | refuse + alternative (`--force-with-lease` to a branch of your own, or push a new branch and open a PR) | rewrites shared history; rev 28 (A80): the lease forms no longer match |
| `git push --force-with-lease` / `--force-if-includes` | refused as a force push (0.15) | allow to a branch of one's own; refuse + alternative to main/master/develop, to the shared checkout's current branch (push to a branch of your own, open a PR), for `--all`/`--mirror`/a pattern (name one branch) and inside eval or a script (write it as one plain git command; `bash -c` and the like are read inside) | A80: a fast route on one's own branch; the shared branch carries every session's commits |
| Push to main/master/develop | ask | refuse + alternative (push your branch, open a PR) | lands unreviewed |
| `git add .` / `-A` / `--all` | ask; refused in every repository (0.15) | refuse + alternative (stage exact paths) in the shared S2 checkout only; runs in a worktree of one's own or another repository | A80: stages other sessions' files only where they share the tree |
| `git switch` / `checkout <branch>` in the shared checkout | ask | refuse + alternative (a worktree of your own) | moves the tree under every session |
| Git that can discard work on exact paths in the shared checkout (`checkout <ref> -- <paths>`, `checkout -- <paths>`, `restore <paths>`, `stash push -- <paths>`, `clean -f -- <paths>`, `rm [-r] <paths>`, from any folder `cd`/`pushd` reached) | ask (whole-tree status, timing out on S2) | check: one path-scoped status (untracked files only for clean); runs when clean or changed only in this session's touch paths that no other live session claims; else refuse naming each path and owner + alternative (commit or move it first, or a worktree of your own); unreadable status → refuse + alternative (fewer, deeper paths, or a worktree) | A74: decided on facts |
| The same discards with no paths, `.`, a whole top folder, a variable or a pattern | ask | refuse + the same command on exact paths (`checkout <ref> -- <paths>`, `restore -- <paths>`, `stash push -- <paths>`, `clean -f -- <paths>`, `rm -- <paths>`) | a whole-tree status takes 36–82 s on S2 (review 1) |
| `git reset --hard` (and `--merge`, `--keep`) in the shared checkout | ask | refuse + alternative (`checkout <ref> -- <paths>`, or a worktree) | rewinds the whole tree; no path form exists |
| `git clean -x` / `-X` in the shared checkout | ask (ran when status was clean) | refuse + alternative (`clean -f -- <paths>` without -x/-X) | Saved/, Intermediate/, Binaries/ are ignored, never in status (review 2) |
| `git checkout -b/-B`, `switch -c/-C` in the shared checkout | unchecked | refuse + alternative (create it in a worktree of your own) | moves the tree; -B resets a branch (review 3) |
| `git stash pop/apply` in the shared checkout | ask (S80: refuse) | check (0.15.1): the stash's own paths (`stash show --name-only`) read like a discard's; refuse naming another session's change | a session's own stash must come back (re-review) |
| `git stash branch` in the shared checkout | ask | refuse + alternative (apply it in a worktree of your own) | moves the tree to a new branch |
| `git checkout <word>` (no `--`) in the shared checkout | ask | an existing file that is no commit → path check (0.15.1); else refuse + alternative (a worktree of your own) | `checkout <file>` is a path checkout (re-review) |
| `git apply -R` in the shared checkout | unchecked | refuse + alternative (`git revert <sha>`, or a worktree) | rewrites files from a patch A5R cannot read |
| `git worktree remove --force` | unchecked | refuse + alternative (commit or move its changes, then remove without --force) | deletes uncommitted changes |
| `git branch -D` (or `-d --force`) | unchecked | refuse + alternative (`git branch -d`, merged branches only) | loses unmerged commits |
| `git submodule update --force` | unchecked | refuse + alternative (without --force) | discards changes inside submodules |
| `git stash drop` / `clear` in the shared checkout | ask | runs when no stash entry exists; else refuse + alternative (leave the stash; keep your work in a commit or a worktree) | an entry may hold another session's work |
| `git config --global` for `credential.*`, `lfs.*`, `core.autocrlf/eol/hooksPath/fsmonitor`, `filter.*` | ask (S79: allow + recorded) | refuse + alternative (`git config --local`, or `git -c <key>=<value>`) | breaks every repository on the machine (review 6) |
| `git config --global`, other keys | ask | allow + recorded (the key), named at nghiệm thu | reversible, this machine only |
| `p4 obliterate` | ask (S79: allow + recorded) | refuse + alternative (`p4 delete`, a recoverable revision) | cannot be undone (review 6) |
| `p4 revert` / `p4 delete` | ask | allow + recorded, named at nghiệm thu | recoverable |
| Recursive delete outside temp folders | ask | allow + recorded (its targets); named at nghiệm thu (rule 5) until progress.md or findings.md gives the reason | A75, review 6 |
| Repository config edit (`*.uproject`, `*.Build.cs`, `*.Target.cs`, `Config/*.ini`, `Plugins/*/Config/*.ini`, `.claude/settings.json`, root `.gitignore`/`.gitattributes`/`AGENTS.md`/`CLAUDE.md`) in the shared checkout | ask | allow + recorded; acceptance rule 3 asks for it in the PR body | A75 |
| Shared agent config (`~/.claude/settings.json`, Hermes `config.yaml`) | ask | allow + recorded | A75 |
| Edit outside a scope file (`rules/scope`) | ask | allow + recorded; acceptance rule 3 already scores scope | A75 |
| Test assertions removed | ask | allow + recorded; acceptance rule 5 asks for the reason | A75 |
| Editor lock written by hand (edit or shell) | refuse | refuse + alternative (the editor tool) | one writer of the lock |
| A coordination notice written into `docs/intent/*` (edit or shell) | refuse any line containing the prefix | refuse + alternative only a line that is a notice (prefix and gate at its start) | A76 |
| Editor use (Unreal MCP write, start/stop) without the lock | refuse | refuse + alternative (request via the editor tool) | another session drives that Editor |
| Editor use under a lock line written by hand (no `session <id8>`) | refuse its own holder too, telling it to "add 'session …' to the lock" (an edit that is refused) | rev 30 (A84, S88): the one session the token after `held by` names (id or lane) runs, releases and re-takes it with the tool; anyone else: refuse + alternative (`editor request`: queued); a name matching two live sessions or none: refuse + alternative (`editor request` converts it to a standard line when no other A5R lease runs and a fresh reading shows no Unreal Editor; no reading → queued with the lapse time); lapses 10 min past its end (holder known and not alive) or 30 min (holder unknown) with nothing running; a line naming nobody after its end or 2 h | A84, orchestrator |
| Unreal MCP call classed as a write by words in its arguments (`set_`, `run_` …) | refuse while another lane holds the Editor | rev 30: classed by the tool's name and method only; a read runs; S88: any console command and a method that can quit, close or save need the lock | review of 0.17.1, re-review |
| Editor save-all | refuse | refuse + alternative (save each package by exact path) | it saves other lanes' packages |
| PIE with free RAM under 5 GB | refuse | refuse + alternative (free memory, then start PIE) | fixed crash gate |
| PIE when the RAM probe fails | allow, silently | rev 30 (A88 c, S88): the probe runs once more; failing again, refuse + alternative (read free RAM with the given command, pass `freeGb` to the editor tool, start PIE again; the number goes through the 5 GB gate, logged) | no blind pass (orchestrator) |
| RAM probe failing while this session's PIE runs | silent | rev 30 (S88): a notice to the holder (read free RAM now, stop PIE under 3 GB) | the 3 GB abort cannot be checked |
| Editor grant when no RAM reading exists | wait `probe`, no end | rev 30 (A88 c, S88): a fresh probe once more; failing again, wait + alternative (read free RAM with the given command, call request again with `freeGb`; the number goes through the launch gate, logged) | no blind pass, no wait without a step |
| Editor request while the tick runs slow work (the score, the judge, the holder's dry-run) | waited on the same chain (1–3 min at a cutoff, MEASURED) | rev 30 (A88 b): answered from the Editor's own pass | not a gate; a stall removed |
| Sync freeze: a non-holder's git writes or Editor use in the shared checkout | refuse | refuse + alternative (work without git or the Editor; told at the lift) | D8 lease, bounded by its hard end |
| Merge guard: git writes while `.git/MERGE_HEAD` exists (non-holder); rev 39 (A115): also `.git/REVERT_HEAD` and `.git/CHERRY_PICK_HEAD`, refused to every session but the owner (the frozen sync's holder, else the session its owner record names, else the last sync's holder) | refuse | refuse + alternative: the live holder finishes it or runs `git merge --abort` (A115: `git revert|cherry-pick --abort`, the operation named); its holder gone or no sync on file: `/a5r sync takeover`, then `git -C <S2> merge --abort` (rev 30, A87: never "or Hai") | a half-done merge, revert or cherry-pick |
| Editor grant over `.git/MERGE_HEAD` | wait | wait (not a refusal of a call) | A61 |
| Editor grant with the lock missing or empty | wait `missing`, no end (nobody may write it under A5R) | rev 30 (F-8, S87): A5R writes the standard FREE line once a fresh probe shows no Unreal Editor; while one runs, wait (held by unknown) until it ends | A73 "never wait"; nothing killed |
| Acceptance at the checklist end (PR call, intent close) | refuse with the list (no dialog since A57) | unchanged; `/a5r pass` lets one through; rev 30 (A86): A5R's own agents (`a5r:judge`, `a5r:sync`) are not "a background agent still running" | ship quality is A5R's job |
| Acceptance: a branch diff that cannot be read | refuse with why (a whole `-U0` over 4 MiB included) | rev 30 (A88 a): a cut whole diff is re-read in path batches; refuse only for a git failure or timeout, or a single file whose own diff passes 4 MiB (named: split it or give it a PR of its own) | an unread diff never passes |
| After-the-fact score of a failing PR | a red mark for Hai (title, unread, list line) | the card line and one Acceptance notice to the session tracking its intent, once per PR (a notice, not a gate) | A83: decisions are Ather's |
| Decision convention: an answer's decision line or waiting line, a sync expiry, a merge left behind | title mark, unread, a list-file line, a "Waiting on Hai" toast (no gate) | removed: nothing marked; the expiry and the merge left behind are one notice each (the holder; every session) | A83 |
| Editor tool requests (release by a non-holder, release with PIE running, extend past the cutoff / after the end with waiters / twice with waiters) | refuse + alternative | unchanged; rev 34 (A102): a second extension while others wait runs when it is the retest (≤ 15 min, no build lease, an Editor running, ≤ 90 min in all, before the end); the refusal names that path | they answer the caller's own request; nothing waits |
| Editor tool: a build window over 45 min | refuse + alternative, even with an empty queue (0.15) | allow when nobody else is queued and no sync cutoff falls inside; refuse + alternative while someone is queued (≤ 45 min) or across a cutoff (the room left) | A81: the S2 standard's cap protects those who wait |
| Sync tool and `/a5r sync` (not the holder, frozen, bad time, sync.json changed) | refuse + alternative | unchanged | the caller's own request |
| Sync tool done without the post-merge test line | ran | rev 32 (A95): refuse + alternative (run the S2 standard headless tests and call done with their result line, or "tests skipped: <why>") | the tests are part of the sync |
| A sync worker that ends mid-build (no SYNC line, or SYNC MERGED) | recorded aborted, the freeze lifted under a running build | rev 32 (A93): git decides: a merge commit keeps the freeze through building and testing; aborted only by an abort or with no merge commit | MEASURED 2026-10-19, six syncs |
| The sync holder's Editor request for its own sync | queued behind others, capped at 45 min | rev 32 (A94): first, uncapped, ending with the sync | the build every waiting lane needs |
| A sync worker's SYNC ABORT, an ending with no merge commit, or a worker that cannot start | aborted (or a notice to run the merge by hand), the sync's lock released and the Editor granted to the next lane | rev 33 (A96): the manual stage: still frozen, the lock kept for the holder (never lapsed or passed on while frozen), at least 30 min left; the holder finishes and calls done, or aborts (the Editor freed at once) | MEASURED 2026-10-19/20: "unable to unlink" under the next lane's Editor |
| `git checkout --ours/--theirs -- <paths>` in the shared checkout | checked as a discard (refused on a changed path no session of this one's claims) | rev 33 (A97): while `.git/MERGE_HEAD` exists the unmerged paths (DD AU UD UA DU AA UU) run; other paths checked as before | resolving a conflict is no discard |
| `git update-index` | a read for the freeze and A15 | rev 33 (A98): a git write (frozen for the others); the sync's own `update-index -q --refresh` passes A15 | it writes the index during the merge |
| Editor tool, Sync tool and the A5R pane with no checkout | `no S2 checkout at <the default checkout> → set the editorLock option …` (the default named one machine's folder) | rev 37 (A111): with `editorLock` unset and no Unreal checkout found from the session's repository: refuse + alternative `no Unreal checkout found for this session (…) → set the a5r option editorLock to <checkout>/Saved/EDITOR_OWNER.txt`; with the option set and its folder missing, as before (`no S2 checkout at <folder>`); the pane says the coordination is idle and how to point it; the five rules unchanged | coordination needs a checkout; no folder of one machine in the plugin |
| Writes into the live a5r plugin folder: the alternative's words | `… a worktree of its repository (D:/Projects/ather-mods-wt/<name>) …` | rev 37 (A111): `… a worktree of its repository (git -C <repository> worktree add <folder of your own> <branch>) …` (the refusal itself unchanged) | no folder of one machine |
| Editor use in a launch:false pass slot (S118: PIE, Unreal MCP writes, console commands and any shell launch, headless included; a stop runs) | none | rev 38 (A113): refuse + alternative under the Editor lock gate (do the work that needs no Editor; to use an Editor, release and request again without launch: false) | the slot went before a head that waits for memory because it launches none; a measured fact (free RAM under that head's gate) |
| `extend` of a pass slot past 30 min in all while its head is queued | the A68/A81/A102 checks | rev 38 (A113): refuse + alternative (finish by its 30 min, release, ask again) | a pass is capped so the head gets the next grant that fits |
| Sync tool `rules` (with plan, move or the `rules` action) | none | rev 33 (A99): refuse + alternative for a line that is not `ours <glob>` / `theirs <glob>` (the form), and for the `rules` action once the worker started (finish by hand or abort) | the worker reads its rules once, in its task |
| Sync worker's calls to A5R's own tools (`mcp__a5r__editor`, `mcp__a5r__sync`) | failed: no hook answered (every sync aborted) | removed: the worker calls none; the holder's a5r takes and releases the lock in code and reads the worker's SYNC DONE / SYNC ABORT line | A79 |
| Cutoff notice (a prompt to every A5R session) | every session, actionable | only the holder and sessions with touched paths or holding/queued for the Editor; others one context line at the freeze, the lift at their next turn | A82 (a notice, not a gate) |
| Sync main panel button (`$.ui.ask`) | Hai opens it | kept | Hai presses it himself; the only dialog left |

## Engineering decisions (inside the intent's scope)

- E1 Grant vs sync: a grant's slot must end by the cutoff (T − 30), which satisfies both A3 ("fits before the next sync cutoff") and A5 ("grants that would cross the sync are deferred"); from the cutoff until done/abort only the sync holder is granted. The wait names the room left ("ask again for ≤ N min").
- E2 Launch gate (D5) applies only when no UnrealEditor runs; a FREE lock with the Editor still open is reused and its memory is already counted. PIE start 5 GB / abort 3 GB stay fixed. A missing RAM reading fails closed (the slot waits).
- E3 Liveness is conservative: a fresh Ather lane vouches for a session even if its hai-flow file is stale; a holder with neither file is unknown and its lease is never freed. Recovery writes `FREE … note=stale lease … background=unknown` (the gone holder cannot assert `none`).
- E4 Every hai-flow session with A5 on keeps a session file (heartbeat) even without a request, so "holder runs hai-flow" and "every A5 session" are file facts.
- E5 Panel presets for a sync: the first half hour ≥ 45 min ahead, then +1 h, +2 h (the cutoff notice still lands before its cutoff).
- E6 Launch gate control: one shared override in the plugin store (every session reads it) over the plugin options; the panel moves both gates together by 1 GB and Reset returns to the options; clamped to 10–60 GB.
- E7 "Main loop" for notices and busy/idle = `agentId === undefined && next.origin.plugin === 'engine'` (a plugin's own `$.tool.call` carries an engine-minted tool_use_id but its own origin).
- E8 Notice actionability: grant, yield, lease end/overrun/idle, gone-holder Editor, PIE abort, cutoff, release-by, holder's run-merge, conflicts, lift → actionable (one idle prompt); recovered lease, disk, freeze (non-holder) → wait for the next turn as context. A lift reaches only sessions told of that sync, within 6 h, and supersedes their pending cutoff/freeze notices.
- E9 Freeze scope: git writes whose repository root is the S2 shared checkout (main working tree), every Unreal MCP call and Editor start/stop; the sync holder and its workers are exempt; reads, other repositories and linked worktrees pass. `cd <worktree> && git …` still counts as the shared checkout (same limit as A5's git rules: use `git -C`).
- E11 (rev 4) Where the paused s2-sync-main skill differs from runbook-sync-lane, the worker follows A12 / the runbook: a binary conflict that is not a self-conflict aborts (the skill said "main wins"), `git merge --abort` is the worker's own step on such a conflict (the skill said report only), and a failed build is reverted with `git revert -m 1` (the skill said do not touch git).
- E12 (rev 4) The worker waits for an Editor another lane holds until 10 min before the hard end, then aborts, so its done/abort lands before the sync expires.
- E13 (rev 4) The freeze's `expired` is computed by every reader (hard end, or holder gone while frozen) and written by whichever session sees it first; one 🟥 per event through an alert file under `Saved/HaiFlow/alerts/` (sync expiry; a merge left behind, keyed by MERGE_HEAD's time). Before T a gone holder is still taken over (A5 behaviour kept).
- E14 (rev 4) The merge guard's "holder" is the holder of the last sync in any state, so it can still finish or abort its own merge after an expiry; everyone else waits for MERGE_HEAD to go.
- E15 (rev 5) Nghiệm thu A5 is triggered by the PR-opening call (gh pr create, gh api …/pulls POST) and by an intent's Status line closing; Ather's Ship hand-off has no observable event, so it is reached through the PR call it makes. Hai's pass-once covers that one call. Debug leftovers in docs (*.md, docs/) are not scored (docs may name console.log).
- E10 Rule-11 classification caps at 40 paths (each costs a rev-parse and a `git log --raw --max-count=200`); the rest count as foreign (the conservative side). No fetch before the dry-run (F-3).
- E16 (rev 18) After the fact, an old scorer's PR is recognised by its alert file (`pr-<n>.json` without `scorer`, not withdrawn): a pre-0.12.3 session file cannot tell the PRs it scored from those it only found listed, and only the scored-and-failed ones raised a 🟥; a PR the old path passed (no alert) is not scored again. The title's 🟥 itself is left to Hai (PENDING line ticked, alert withdrawn).
- E17 (rev 18) A merged or closed PR is read from `gh pr diff`, not from local refs: after a merge commit `origin/<base>...origin/<head>` is empty, which would pass it unread.
- E18 (rev 18) A single path longer than the card's text width (e.g. `.agents/skills/explicit-commit-pr/` at a 40-column desktop pane) can only break inside the path; the A53 test checks that items wrap and that each rule's name row fits, not that a path fits unbroken.
- E19 (rev 20) The release needs a fresh probe that names no Unreal Editor process at all, not only the lock's own pid: the lock line may say `pid=none` while an Editor runs (launched outside the lock); such a lease keeps its overrun notice and stays to Hai.
- E20 (rev 20) A55's "seen" is the holder's own `lastTurnAt` (its a5r writes it at turns, not at the minute timer) or its Ather lane's `lastActiveAt`; a holder whose a5r writes no turn time (before 0.13.0) is never judged unseen; A54 still releases its lease once lapsed. An unseen grant is passed on only while another session waits.
- E21 (rev 20) The releaser writes the holder's session file (its only write to another session's file): the holder may be gone, and the queue reads `holding`; a live holder's own a5r reads the `released` record at its next minute, drops its lease and request, and rewrites its file.
- E22 (rev 21) A PR enters acceptance by the checklist of the intent its diff is for, not always the tracked one: a PR opened from another worktree for another intent (A41's avatar-frame case) is judged by that intent's rows; a tracked intent is still required (none tracked: the PR runs). A47 (a)'s "most-named intent" and tie now show on demand (`/a5r accept`) only.
- E23 (rev 21) Before the checklist ends, after-the-fact scoring records the listed PR numbers as known, so a PR opened during Build is not scored the moment the last row turns met; only PRs listed after the end are.
- E24 (rev 22) Not fixed in this rev (from the review's list, no row): the lock line's pid is written only at the grant (`pid` of an Editor already open) and at an extension (`lock.pid ?? editorPid(probe)`); an Editor launched after the grant leaves `pid=none`, so "pid=none" does not prove no Editor ran (A58 checks every Unreal process instead). An extension is refused once the lease has ended, so a live holder past its end can only release and ask again.
- E25 (rev 22) A62's candidate lines are logged once per lease and rule by the first session to claim them (alert files `log-…`), so concurrent sessions do not repeat a line; appends are read-modify-write, so two sessions appending in the same instant can lose one line (accepted for a measurement log). "Running agents" is the holder's own count from its session file (absent for an a5r older than 0.13.1).
- E26 (rev 23) The Orchestrate card sits just above the five rules, not below them: A27 keeps the rules as the pane's last block (its tests stand).
- E27 (rev 23) The judge's verdict is not computed by the test world (no model runs there): `hintOf` gives a deterministic first reading from the facts and the transcript, which the tests check on fixture transcripts, which the judge is told to confirm or correct, and which stands in when no judge can start or its answer is unreadable (marked `(rule)`). A verdict whose agent answers after a reload of this session is lost (the agent-to-incident map is in memory); the incident is not judged twice (its claim file stays).
- E28 (rev 23) The judge runs only in a session other than the holder (a stalled session would not run its own judge); with no other A5R session open, nobody judges.
- E29 (rev 24) My reading of the three replay cases from their transcripts (for the orchestrator's comparison): fluidninja-live2-upgrade: not working; its last event is 19:18, a `git push` call that never returned, so the turn was stopped long before the 20:44 grant, which it never saw. mc-dash-sprint-carry: done; it built (15:48, `Result: Succeeded`), tested (15/15), ran PIE, then closed the Editor and wrote the FREE line itself at 16:15, and was idle until 18:36; a lock still showing its 15:49–17:15 lease at 17:25 would be stale. 1007-filler-section3: working through its background worker: the main session's last turn was 16:35 (it relayed Hai's "the wall is too small" to worker ae3a6242, which held the Editor to 17:20 and was building the wall proof map); a rule that looked only at the main loop would call it idle, which A67 now prevents.
- E30 (rev 37) With no checkout found, A5R's own folder (`hfDir()`) is `<TEMP>/a5r` (TEMP, else TMPDIR, else /tmp), not a null: the coordination paths stay gated at their entries (the minute tick runs only when the session's root holds the checkout's .uproject; the editor and sync tools check the checkout first), so the folder serves only the rules' own records and alerts (A23's after-the-fact PR alerts are claimed once among the machine's sessions without a checkout), and anything that slipped would land in a private temp folder, never at a drive root. A null folder would need handling at about six acceptance and freeze readers. A75's recorded actions are still written only into a checkout that exists (as before on a machine without the configured checkout).
- E31 (rev 37) `under(path, '')` is false: an empty folder (no checkout) holds nothing. Before, a posix path (`/…`) counted as inside it; on Windows nothing changes (`E:/…` never started with `/`).
- E32 (rev 37) The option counts wherever it points (a folder without a .uproject, or not on this machine: its tools then say `no S2 checkout at <folder>` as before); only detection needs a .uproject at the main tree's root. A session coordinates itself (the minute tick, the tiles) when its root holds the checkout's .uproject (was: S2.uproject), so a session outside the checkout with the option set uses the tools and the gates but runs no tick, as before.
- E33 (rev 37) The engine root: the option counts only with its Build.bat (A110: "a candidate counts only when its Build.bat exists"), so a wrong option is passed over and named in the task rather than fatal; the sources are read in order and the reading stops at the first Build.bat; the log is read only from `<project>.log` and its backups (MEASURED: the newest *.log by time is often cef3.log or a test run's); the build target is `<project>Editor` from the checkout's .uproject (S2Editor for S2).
- E34 (rev 38) A113's "fits" is decide's own grant for the later request's slot capped at 30 min, so a pass meets every check the head would (merge in progress, a planned cutoff, a sync's cutoff or freeze, the launch gate); "a lower gate" follows: the head failed its gate on the same reading, the passer met its own. Only the first that fits in queue order passes (one at a time). The age is counted from the head's requestedAt (a kept A104 place counts from its original request).
- E35 (rev 38) A114's memory note lives in the session's own editor step, not nudge.ts: the facts are its own (its decision `ram`, its turns, running calls and agents, the probe), delivery is a notice to itself through deliverIdle like the grant, and no claim, inbox or transcript read is needed; nudge.ts serves sessions with no request, whose waiting is only inferred from their transcript, and now holds the one idle rule both use.
- E36 (rev 38) The probe groups every process by name (`top`, 6 heaviest) beside its named list, so "memory is held by" names claude.exe and Chrome; the named list keeps feeding the cleanup, build and Editor checks unchanged. The wait line's note uses keyed words (`freeGb`, `gateGb`) because the log's note filter (`safeNote`) rewrites a bare "free". orchestrate.log is rewritten whole by each writer (the engine has no append): two sessions writing in the same instant can lose one line; the next 15-min line or change re-states the wait.
- E37 (rev 38, S118) The Editor shell classifier reads programs, not text: a command counts as a launch or a stop only when a program it runs (wrappers stepped over, a shell's own command read) launches or stops an UnrealEditor binary; text that names one (an echo, a read, a diff, a build's target) no longer needs the Editor lock (it did under the regex). A pipe into Stop-Process is not read (as before); a launch it misses shows in the minute's probe.
- E38 (rev 39, S119) A115's "session that started it" is a record of its own (`Saved/A5R/head-owner.json`), written by that session's a5r after its merge, pull, revert or cherry-pick in the shared checkout left a head file, and tied to that file's mtime: a record names nobody for any later head file, so a stale one never outlives its operation, and no liveness or time window is needed. Only those four commands write it (a commit running while another session's cherry-pick stops does not take ownership). A takeover writes the same record (`took over`) instead of rewriting sync.json's holder, so the guard has one owner rule for the three head files.
- E39 (rev 39, S120) A head file is claimed only by the command that can have left it (`OPS.leftBy`), written after that command started, and never over a valid record; a backgrounded operation waits on process facts (seen, then gone) rather than a time window alone. Ownership needs liveness: only a coordinating session (one with a session file and a tick) is ever recorded owner, so every owner can be taken over once gone.
